```text

教训：**断言要读「人看到的那一整块」，不是读「我以为那句话所在的元素」**。
改成读整栏（`textOf("compare")`）之后，一个断言同时看到了状态与说明，
而且以后再往这一栏里加字，它不会因为「字换了个位置」假红。


## F69. 「同一件事算两次」的失败不是崩溃，是两个都对但不一样（2026-10-27）

C2 历史仪表盘要做判定分布与类别短板。这两件事在项目里已经各有一份实现：`compare.ts` 的 `verdictCounts`
与 `shortfall.ts` 的 `categoryShortfalls`——报告里「站得住 N 条」与「最集中的是技能掌握」就是它们算的。
仪表盘完全可以自己再 `reduce` 一遍，写起来更快，也不会立刻出问题。问题是它会在**某一次改口径之后**出问题：
比如「不同意的判定算不算欠证据」这种定义换个地方写，两份数字就分叉，而两边看起来都很合理。

处置：`packages/shared/src/overview.ts` 直接 import 那两个函数，自己不持有任何「哪个判定更差」的知识；
仪表盘的结论与报告的结论同源，e2e 里还加了一条断言把界面文字与接口 JSON 逐字对齐。
这与 F27（跨文档数字）是同一类失效：**同一事实允许有多个读者，不允许有多个作者**。

## F70. 数据层永远给结论，界面层决定什么时候值得说（2026-10-27）

`overviewSessions` 对空库也返回完整结构（headline 是「还没有跑过任何一场。」），从不返回 `null`；
而 `OverviewBar` 在 `facts.empty` 时整块不渲染。第一版我们两边做反了：接口对空库返回 null，
界面每一处都要写 `overview ? ... : null`，加一个字段就要改三个地方；
而且「空」和「还没取到」在类型上混成一种状态，刷新时会闪一下「还没有跑过任何一场」。

分开之后：`null` 只表示「还没问过服务端」，空库是一个**有明确含义的正常结果**。
这条规则与 C1 的 `CompareView`（第一版 / 上一版被删 / 正常对比都是 200，不是 404）是同一条。
```

```text
30: | 反推 | 定位到原文片段的简历修改建议 |
31: | 短板 | 同一批判定换个角度：按主张类别聚合，先排欠得最多的那一类，再列出最该补的 3 处，点一下跳回简历原文 |
32: | 强度 | **追问强度**三档：温和 / 常规 / 挑刺。换的是预算（每主张 2 / 3 / 4 轮、全场 40 / 60 / 80 次）不是人设，档位随会话留档 |
33: | 风格 | **改写风格**三档：保守（只做减法）/ 常规 / 敢改（整句重写）。只调措辞收敛程度，三条红线不动：不加简历里没有的数字、不把参与写成主导、改前原文必须锚得回原文 |
34: | 补证据 | 任何一条主张上可以补一句真话当作新证据；采纳时它作为新的一行进简历，原文一个字不动 |
35: | 反问 | 跑完给你几句能反问 HR 的话，每句都必须锚回简历原话或 JD 原文，锚不回的当场丢弃；回答明确标注是沙盘模拟 |
36: | 改稿 | 勾选想采纳的建议 → **确认后自动改简历**，右栏直接换成改完的版本；改动段标绿，改不动的（引文在原简历里锚不住）会说明理由。点「并排看改动」还能逐条对照改前改后，并看到它们落在简历的什么位置 |
37: | 带走 | 报告三种形态：Markdown / 可打印 HTML（另存为 PDF）/ Word；改完的简历另外三种：`.txt` / 导出 PDF / 导出 Word |
38: | 复跑 | **改完再跑一遍**：采纳改写后点「改完再跑一遍」，它拿改完的简历重开一场并记下父会话，逐条告诉你哪些主张的判定变好了（按文本相似度把两场的主张配上对，配不上的单独摆出来）；两份报告开头也会多一节「比上次」 |
39: | 回顾 | **历史仪表盘**：历史栏顶上一句话结论（几场、多少条主张、站得住多少、最欠证据的类别、平均每场追问几次），加最近 5 场的趋势柱与「同一份简历跑过几场」——跑到第三场之后就靠它看出是在变好还是在打转 |
40: | 模式 | 页面里**逐场**选运行模式：离线回放（默认，不联网）/ 联网直连 / 联网录制；模型名可下拉可手填，没配好就直接告诉你缺哪个环境变量 |
41: | 上手 | 第一次打开自动弹一次新手教程：四步用起来、三种模式对照、隐私口径、常见问题 |
```

```text
## 3.7 工程质量：门禁四道

`npm run verify` 是提交前必须全绿的一道门，它由四件事组成：

| 顺序 | 命令 | 证明什么 | 当前规模 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | `npm run typecheck` | 类型对（`tsc --noEmit`，含各包） | 全仓库 |
| 2 | `npm run test` | 逻辑对（vitest） | 67 个文件 / 691 条断言 |
| 3 | `npm run smoke` | **产品能跑**（真起进程、真打 HTTP、真等 SSE） | 9 项 |
| 4 | `npm run build` | 前端能构建 | vite，46 modules（CSS 29.69 kB / JS 344.53 kB，gzip 105.62 kB） |

第 3 道是`findings.md` F11 的直接产物：同进程的 `app.request()` 测试全绿，真起服务却第一次写入就报没有表。
**单元测试证明「函数逻辑对」，冒烟证明「产品能跑」——两句话之间隔着一个进程边界，而事故恰好长在边界上。**

除 verify 之外还有三个可选门禁，都做成了命令：

| 命令 | 作用 | 什么时候跑 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `npm run shots` | 无头浏览器点一遍全流程，出 24 张截图，**控制台有报错即失败** | 改了界面之后 |
| `npm run repro` | 干净目录 clone → install → verify → 起服跑一场，逐步计时 | 改复现路径之后 |
| `npm run seed:demo` | 重新生成 149 盘离线卡带并自检 | 改了提示词之后 |
```

```text
# 发布清单（提交省赛前的逐项核对）

> 用法：从仓库根目录自上而下走一遍，每一项都能勾掉才提交。
> 标 ⛔ 的是**只有参赛者本人能决定**的事项，AI 无法代办。

## 一、赛题要求的五项提交物

| # | 要求 | 现状 | 位置 / 命令 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | GitHub / Gitee 公开仓库，**细粒度 commit**（禁止一次性 initial commit） | ✅ 300 余次提交，每次对应一次具体开发动作 | 见第二节「建远端」 |
| 2 | **≥3 个核心功能迭代的完整 Prompt 链**（提问 → AI 建议 → 如何引导修 Bug） | ✅ 自动导出，34 份文件（其中 30 份分页快照，约 14 MB） | `npm run export:prompt` → `docs/prompt-chain/` |
| 3 | README 10 分钟极速复现指南（环境、依赖、环境变量模板） | ✅ 干净目录实测 70.8 s（同机多次落在 34–121 s） | `README.md` + `.env.example` |
| 4 | 演示视频 5–8 分钟 1080p，含 **≥2 分钟 AI 协同过程复现** | 🔶 脚本已备，**待录制** | `docs/video/script.md` |
| 5 | 技术文档 PDF ≤30 页 | ✅ 28 页（上限 30，余量 2 页；构建自身在超限时失败），一条命令重建 | `npm run docs:pdf` → `docs/CrossExam-技术文档.pdf` |

## 二、建远端仓库（⛔ 需要本人操作）

**为什么是 ⛔**：仓库地址与账号归属只有参赛者能决定，AI 拿不到也不该代填。

1. 在 GitHub 或 Gitee 新建**公开**仓库（不要初始化 README，本地已有）。
2. 在仓库根执行（把地址换成真实地址）：

```
git remote add origin <仓库地址>
git push -u origin main
```

3. **推送后立刻核对三件事**：
   - 贡献者列表里只有本人，没有 AI 服务商账号（本仓库提交信息从不写 AI 署名尾注，见 AGENTS.md 第 2.3 节）。
   - `.env` 没有被推上去（`git ls-files | grep -c '^\.env$'` 应为 0）。
   - 仓库首页能直接渲染 README（含截图）。

4. 把真实仓库地址填回 `README.md` 顶部的 `<仓库地址>` 占位，再提交一次（`docs: 填入公开仓库地址`）。
```

```text
  return round4(shared / union);
}

/** 牵起来的一对：上一场的某条 ↔ 这一场的某条 */
export interface AlignedPair {
  prev: JudgedClaimLike;
  next: JudgedClaimLike;
  similarity: number;
}

export interface ClaimAlignment {
  pairs: AlignedPair[];
  /** 只在这一场出现：新加的、或者上一场被拆成了两条 */
  added: JudgedClaimLike[];
  /** 只在上一场出现：删掉的、或者改写后已经不像同一条 */
  removed: JudgedClaimLike[];
}

/**
 * 一对一贪心牵线。
 *
 * 为什么不是最优匹配（匈牙利算法那类）：主张数量是个位数到十几条，
 * 贪心已经能得到同样的结果，而且**顺序是确定的**——先看最像的一对。
 * 换成最优匹配反而多一处需要解释的实现，报告的结论还得再解释一遍。
 * 排序的兜底键是两边原始下标，保证同样的输入永远得到同样的配对。
 */
export function alignClaims(
  prev: readonly JudgedClaimLike[],
  next: readonly JudgedClaimLike[],
  threshold: number = DEFAULT_ALIGN_THRESHOLD,
): ClaimAlignment {
  const candidates: { prevIndex: number; nextIndex: number; similarity: number }[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < prev.length; i += 1) {
    const left = prev[i];
    if (left === undefined) continue;
    for (let j = 0; j < next.length; j += 1) {
      const right = next[j];
      if (right === undefined) continue;
      const similarity = textSimilarity(left.text, right.text);
      if (similarity < threshold) continue;
      candidates.push({ prevIndex: i, nextIndex: j, similarity });
----
export fun
```

Design for 成长档案:
- shared: `packages/shared/src/growth.ts` — pure aggregation over a list of session snapshots sharing the same resumeHash... wait, resumeHash changes when resume changes. Hmm: "同一份简历" — same resume text. But if you改完再跑, resume changes. So growth across a version chain (parent_session_id) makes more sense: follow the chain.

Actually two views:
1. 同一份简历（resumeHash 相同）反复跑 → 判定分布趋势 (C2 already has trend for all sessions).
2. 版本链（parent_session_id）→ 每一版简历的判定变化。

成长档案 = 把同一份简历的多次运行 + 版本链串起来，得到每条主张的判定历史。But claim text changes across versions. Use alignClaims to trace a claim across a chain of sessions (C1's alignClaims already does pairwise).

Design: `growthOf(chain)` where chain is sessions ordered oldest→newest, each with claims. For each claim in the latest session, trace back through pairwise alignment to get its verdict history. Output: `ClaimHistory { id, text, latestKind, path: {sessionId, createdAt, claimId, kind|null}[] }`, plus `categoryTrend` (per category, solid ratio over time), plus headline.

That's a nice reuse: alignClaims chained.

Server: `GET /api/sessions/:id/growth` — walk up parent_session_id chain from the given session (bounded), also include sessions with the same resumeHash? Let's keep it to the version chain (parent links) — that's the "改完再跑一遍" lineage, which is exactly what we built. Plus, for sessions with no parent, growth of a single session = just itself.

Hmm but "同一份简历反复跑" (same resume, multiple sessions without parent links) is also growth. Let me do: chain = walk parents, then append other sessions with the same resumeHash as the latest? That's muddled.

Simpler and defensible: 成长档案 follows the version chain (parent_session_id), oldest first. If there's no parent, it says "这是这条链的第一版；改完再跑一遍之后，这里会出现每一条主张的判定怎么变". And separately add: same resumeHash runs count (C2 already computed resumeGroups). Good.

Then claims: for each session in chain, listClaims → JudgedClaimLike. Trace: start from the last session's claims; for each, walk backwards aligning prev→next.

Implementation in shared:
```ts
export interface GrowthSession { sessionId: string; createdAt: string; jobTitle: string; claims: readonly JudgedClaimLike[] }
export interface GrowthStep { sessionId: string; createdAt: string; claimId: string | null; kind: VerdictKind | null }
export interface ClaimHistory { id: string; text: string; category: ClaimCategory; latest: VerdictKind|null; steps: GrowthStep[]; shift: ShiftKind; first: VerdictKind|null }
export interface CategoryGrowth { category; label; runs: {sessionId, solid, judged}[]; latestSolid; delta }
export interface GrowthView (pure): sessions, histories, improved, worsened, categoryTrend, headline, note
```

For each claim in the newest session, walk back: cur = claim in session[i]; find pair in alignClaims(prevClaims, curClaims) where pair.next.id === cur.id → prev claim id. Continue.

steps should be oldest→newest, with null for sessions where the claim didn't exist (added later).

Category trend: for each category, per session: judged count & solid count among claims of that category in that session (using each session's own claims). Simple.

```text
/**
 * 按类别看短板（P10 · A3）。
 *
 * 判定结果原本只能一条一条读：十几条主张、十几个徽章，看完了还是不知道「我最该改哪儿」。
 * 这一层把同一份判定换个角度摆——先按主张类别分组，再按「欠证据的程度」排出最该补的几处。
 * 它只做聚合，不引入任何新判定：输入是什么判定，输出就是什么判定。
 *
 * 全是纯函数、同样输入同样输出（报告与界面共用这一份，回放也依赖它稳定）。
 */
import type { ClaimCategory, VerdictKind } from "./domain.ts";
import { CLAIM_CATEGORY_LABEL, VERDICT_LABEL } from "./domain.ts";

/**
 * 只取聚合真正用到的字段。
 * 服务端的 ClaimRow 与前端的 ClaimView 都能直接喂进来——两边各自多带的字段会被忽略。
 */
```

Now let's plan P12. The roadmap's P12 = 大功能② 成长档案（跨场次能力曲线）+ ③ 一岗一版简历生成.

Given feature freeze 2026-11-08 and today 2026-10-27, plenty of time. But we should be pragmatic: C2's overview already gives resumeGroups. C1 gives compare. So P12 ② 成长档案 can be built on top: for a given resume hash group, show the verdict timeline across sessions — per-claim category curve over runs.

③ 一岗一版简历生成: given JD requirements + claims that passed, produce a tailored resume version — select most relevant claims, order by requirement match, export. This is significant.

Let me think about scope and effort. We have lots of time budget presumably. Let's do 成长档案 first (smaller, builds on C1/C2), then 一岗一版.

Actually, let me reconsider what delivers most value for the competition: 演示视频 needs a narrative: ① 看岗位要什么 → ③ 还差什么怎么补 → ② 改完再跑看进步. ③ (一岗一版简历) is the most easily understood by judges. ② builds on C1/C2.

Both are worth doing. Let me start with 成长档案 (P12-②) since data is nearly there, then 一岗一版 (P12-③).

Hmm, but which order? Roadmap lists ②③ . Let's do ② first as planned.

Design for 成长档案 (growth timeline):
- shared: `growth.ts` — input: sessions of the same resume hash (each with claims judged), output: a timeline of runs with per-category solid ratio, plus per-claim tracking (which claims repeatedly fail).
- Server: `GET /api/sessions/:id/growth` or `GET /api/resumes/:hash/growth`? Simpler: `GET /api/sessions/:id/growth` — returns the resume group that this session belongs to, with all runs in the group ordered by time.
- Web: a panel in history area or a bar showing "同一份简历跑过 3 场" — clicking opens growth view.

Wait — C2's overview already has resumeGroups (resumeHash, sessions, latestId, latestCreatedAt). Growth can extend: per group, per-run category stats.

Let me design carefully but keep it small and coherent:

`packages/shared/src/growth.ts`:
```ts
export interface GrowthRun {
  sessionId: string;
  createdAt: string;
  jobTitle: string;
  difficulty: string;  // maybe
  verdicts: VerdictCounts;
  categories: CategoryShortfall[];
}
```
Hmm, but to keep "同一件事只算一次", growth should reuse `verdictCounts` and `categoryShortfalls`.

Growth value-add over C2: **cross-run curve** — for the same resume, how solid-count changes over runs, and which categories persistently fail.

Key decision: growth is keyed by resumeHash. Sessions in same group = same resume text. C1's rerun changes resume → different hash → different group. So growth tracks "same resume, multiple runs" (e.g. different JD, different difficulty), which is the "is my resume consistently weak in category X" question.

Also valuable: **cross-session claim persistence** — using alignClaims to chain claims across runs, find claims that failed in every run ("老是被打回的那几句"). That's a compelling feature: 「你这句话跑了 3 场，3 场都被追穿」.

That's a real feature with substance and reuses alignClaims. Let me design:

```ts
export interface GrowthRunInput {
  sessionId: string;
  createdAt: string;
  jobTitle: string;
  claims: readonly JudgedClaimLike[];
  probes?: number | null;
}

export interface GrowthRun {
  sessionId, createdAt, jobTitle, verdicts: VerdictCounts, categories..., probes
}

export interface StubbornClaim {
  text: string;
  runs: number;         // 这条主张在几场里出现过
  weakRuns: number;     // 其中几场没站住
  firstSessionId: string;
  lastSessionId: string;
  lastVerdict: VerdictKind | null;
  path: string[];       // 每场的判定中文
}

export interface GrowthView {
  runs: GrowthRun[];      // 时间升序
  trend: ...              // solid 曲线
  stubborn: StubbornClaim[];  // 反复被打回
  headline: string;
}
```

Chaining across runs: start with the first run's claims, then align each subsequent run's claims to the accumulated set (by text similarity). Keep it simple: chain by aligning run i to run i-1's claims? That could break chains. Better: maintain a list of "threads", each thread has latest text; align new run's claims to threads.

Let me write `trackClaims(runs: readonly GrowthRunInput[]): ClaimThread[]`.

Algorithm:
- threads: { id, texts: string[] (per run index or sparse), firstRun, lastRun, verdicts: (VerdictKind|null)[], occurrences: number }
- For each run: align run claims against threads' latest texts using alignClaims(n thread-likes, run claims). Unmatched run claims become new threads. Threads not matched get their verdicts pushed null for this run.
- Result: threads sorted by occurrences desc, weakRuns desc.

Requirements: runs must be ≥ 2 for threads to be meaningful.

This is a good, testable pure function. Then server endpoint `GET /api/sessions/:id/growth` (session → its resume group), web panel.

Effort: moderate. Let's go.

```text
# P11 设计评审：改与复用（B1 / B2 / C1 / C2 / C3）

> 按 AGENTS.md 第 7.1 条：开工前先给一页评审。本页写清「解决谁的什么问题 / 怎么做 / 风险 / 怎么验收」。
> 评审通过后动代码；越界改动重新评审。

## 一、这次要解决什么

用户（求职者）的闭环现在停在「跑完一场 → 勾选采纳 → 导出一版改完的简历」。还差五件事：

1. 改前改后只有一张清单，看不出「这句从什么变成了什么」（B2）
2. 改写口径只有一档：想要更保守或更大胆的人没有选择（B1）
3. 改完再跑一遍，两场结果互不关联，看不到「上次被追问的那三条，这次站住了」（C1）
4. 历史栏只有场次列表，没有趋势（C2）
5. 想把这一场发给别人看，得发四个文件（C3）

## 二、五项的做法与验收

### B2 逐条并排的改动对照（先做；不碰提示词、不碰数据库）

- **怎么做**：`packages/shared/src/revise.ts` 已有的 `applyRevisions` / `movedSpans` 之上加一个纯函数 `sideBySide(applied)`，

  界面 `packages/web/src/components/RevisionBar.tsx` 加一个「并排看」开关，数据直接复用 `GET /api/sessions/:id/revisions` 的 `applied`。
- **风险**：低。不动提示词，卡带与指纹都不受影响。
- **怎么验收**：shared 单测 4 条（无改动 / 多条改动 / 重叠改动 / 坐标越界）；两种报告格式都能看到逐条对齐；e2e 断言开关切换后左右两栏都出现原文与改后。
```

Today is 2026-10-27. Freeze line 2026-11-08. So ~12 days. But practically the AI works fast.

Let me design P12:

1. **成长档案 (Growth profile)**: reuse `resumeGroups` from overview — for a given resume hash group, show a timeline of sessions: each session's verdict distribution, and per-claim evolution across sessions. So `GET /api/sessions/:id/profile` or maybe a shared module `packages/shared/src/growth.ts` that takes a list of SessionFacts ordered chronologically and produces claim-level trajectories: which claims appear/change verdicts across runs.

Actually C1 already does pairwise compare. Growth profile = multi-run trajectory. Key module: `growth.ts` with `claimTrajectories(sessions)` — chain-align consecutive sessions to track a claim across N runs (via text similarity). Reuse `alignClaims`.

Design: `growthCurve(runs)` where runs are chronological `{sessionId, createdAt, jobTitle, claims}`. Produce:
- `line: string` headline
- `rail: GrowthRun[]` with solid/judged counts
- `tracks: ClaimTrack[]` — each track is a chain of `{sessionId, claimId, text, verdict}` across runs, keyed by the first appearance, with `trend: 'up'|'flat'|'down'|'mixed'`, and `runs: number`.
- `categories` per-run category shortfall evolution.

Frontend: a GrowthPane showing tracks (one row per claim chain with colored cells per run). That's a nice visual.

2. **一岗一版简历 (one resume per job)**: given a session's JD requirement matches + claims + verdicts, produce a "job-targeted resume draft": pick claims that are solid and hit requirements, order by relevance, and output a resume text — but the hard rule: **don't invent**. So it's a re-ordering + selection of *existing* resume lines (the anchors), maybe with the accepted revisions applied. That's honest: "把站得住、且对得上这个岗位要求的那几句，按岗位相关性重新排一版".

So: `packages/shared/src/tailor.ts` → `tailorResume(input)` producing `{title, sections: [{heading, lines: [{text, claimId, anchors, requirementIds}]}], excluded: [{claimId, reason}], headline}`. Output as markdown text for export (txt / PDF / Word via existing HTML route).

Implementation approach: keep it deterministic (no LLM) — pure function over claims + requirements + matches + revised resume. Rules:
- Only claims with verdict solid (or at least not unsupported?) — must be strict: only `solid`.
- Actually claim anchors give resume lines; we reorder resume *lines* per section. Hmm, the resume is plain text; segmenting gives outline. Simpler: the tailored resume = a filtered view: keep only lines of the resume that are covered by solid claims, plus structural lines (name/title/section headings), and prepend a short "岗位相关" section listing the requirement hit lines. But dropping lines might remove content the user wants.

Better and honest: output a "岗位版简历" as a document with sections:
1. 抬头（原简历第一段）
2. 与这个岗位最相关的几条（命中要求的主张原文句，按要求编号排序）
3. 其余站得住的主张原文句
4. 略去的：判定为不够站得住、或与岗位无关的主张 —— 逐条说明为什么略去

And it's explicitly a "草稿/建议", not a rewrite. Export as markdown/txt + HTML/Word via existing renderer.

This is deterministic, no LLM, no cassettes. 

Then P13: CI + performance budget + full regression. P14: freeze + video + final.

```text
# AGENTS.md — 工程约定

> 任何 AI 助手 / 协作者在动手前**先读这一页**。这里的规则优先于个人偏好。

## 1. 项目是什么

CrossExam（质询室）：多 Agent 对抗式面试沙盘。详见 [task_plan.md](task_plan.md)。

## 2. 提交规范（**参赛硬要求，最容易被忽视**）

赛题明文禁止「一次性 initial commit 包含全部功能」，要求
**每次提交对应一次 AI 核心对话或一个功能模块**。因此：

### 2.1 提交粒度
- 一个提交 = **一个可独立说明的改动**。宁可拆小，不可合大。
- 禁止 `git add -A && git commit` 式的大杂烩。
- 新增一个模块 → 一个提交；修正该模块的一个 Bug → **另一个**提交。
- 每次提交前，先问：**这个提交对应哪一次 AI 对话？** 答不上来就说明粒度不对。

### 2.2 提交信息格式（Conventional Commits）
```
<type>(<scope>): <中文简述>

<为什么这么改，而不是改了什么——改了什么是 diff 的事>
```
- type：`feat` / `fix` / `refactor` / `docs` / `test` / `chore` / `perf`
- scope：包名，如 `server` / `web` / `shared` / `tools` / `docs`
- 中文简述，不超过 50 字。

### 2.3 署名规则
- **禁止**添加 `Co-Authored-By:` / `Generated-by:` / `Assisted-by:` 等 AI 署名尾注，
  也不要写 AI 服务商邮箱。GitHub 会把它们解析成账号并计入贡献者列表。
- 确有需要说明 AI 参与了某次改动，**写在提交正文的普通句子里**。
- AI 协同的正式证据是 `docs/prompt-chain/` 下的 Prompt 链快照，不是 commit trailer。

## 3. 安全边界

- **禁止**提交任何真实密钥。`.env` 已被 .gitignore 挡住；只提交 `.env.example`。
- 新增任何外发请求前，先确认目标域名，并保证 API Key 只从环境变量读取。
- 用户的简历内容属于个人隐私数据：只落本地 SQLite，**不上传任何第三方存储**。
```

**P12 = 大功能 ② 成长档案（跨场次能力曲线）+ ③ 一岗一版简历**

② 成长档案: builds on C1 (version compare) + C2 (overview). It's about a single person's claims across many sessions: 每条主张的判定怎么变、哪个类别总被打回. Since resumeHash grouping exists in C2, the natural unit is "同一份简历的一组场次". But when you adopt revisions, the resume changes so hash changes. So actually the chain follows parentSessionId! C1 already has the version chain (parent_session_id). So 成长档案 = follow the parent chain from the latest session back to the root, showing each version's verdicts, per-claim evolution, and category trends.

Key design: 
- `packages/shared/src/growth.ts` — pure functions: given a chain of RunSnapshot (ordered oldest→newest), produce:
  - `CurvePoint` per session: version index, createdAt, judged, solid, weak, probes
  - `ClaimTrail`: for each claim lineage across versions — but claims aren't identified across versions except by text alignment (alignClaims). Chain alignment: align v1→v2, v2→v3, and produce trails. That's the interesting bit: a claim's verdict history across versions.
  - `CategoryTrend`: category → solid rate per version.
  - `weakestPersistent`: categories that stay weak across all versions（"总被打回"）.
- Server: `GET /api/sessions/:id/growth` — walk parent chain from the given session to the root (via parent_session_id), assemble facts, call growthOf.
- Web: a "成长档案" panel — where? Interface is already 4 columns and full. Use a modal/drawer (existing pattern: HelpPanel, InterviewerStudio are overlays). So a "成长档案" button in the header or in the history area that opens an overlay panel.

- Crucially: must not invent facts. Implementation: from the resume text, select the segments/bullets corresponding to the chosen claims (via anchors) + keep header/education, and produce a new resume text. Growth design:

Input: `GrowthRun[]` ordered oldest→newest, each `{sessionId, createdAt, jobTitle, claims: JudgedClaimLike[], probes: number|null, resumeHash}`.

Output:
```ts
interface GrowthPoint { sessionId; createdAt; version: number; judged; solid; weak; probes: number|null; }
interface ClaimStep { sessionId; version; claimId; text; verdict; shift: ShiftKind }  // shift relative to previous step
interface ClaimTrail { key: string; text: string; steps: ClaimStep[]; appearedAt: number; vanishedAt: number|null }
interface CategoryTrend { category; label; points: {version; total; solid}[]; persistentWeak: number }
interface GrowthReport { versions: number; points: GrowthPoint[]; trails: ClaimTrail[]; persistent: ClaimTrail[]; categories: CategoryTrend[]; improved: number; regressed: number; headline: string; note: string|null }
```

Trail building: pairwise align consecutive versions with alignClaims (threshold default). Build lineage by union-find over (version, claimId) nodes: edge between prev node and next node when aligned. Then each connected component is a trail; steps sorted by version. Claims unmatched in the next version get vanishedAt = version index of last appearance; new ones appearedAt.

Persistent weak: trails whose every judged step has verdict !== 'solid' and steps ≥ 2 — "总被打回". That's the killer feature: 「你改了三次，这一条还是被判欠证据」.

Headline: e.g. 「3 版：站得住 1 → 2 → 4 条（共 12 条主张），全程被追问的有 2 条，最稳的短板是「技能掌握」。」

Also `solidRate` per category per version.

```text
/**
 * 简历版本对比（P11 · C1）。
 *
 * ¤改完再跑一遍¤ 这句话本身不需要任何新机制——建一场新会话就行。
 * 难的是让评委看见**改动的效果**：同一处主张，上一场判成什么、这一场判成什么。
 * 主张 id（C1..Cn）每场都从 1 重新编号，跨场次毫无意义——
 * 上一场的 C1 是订单重构，这一场的 C1 可能是某张证书。所以这一层只做一件事：
 * 用文本相似度把两场的主张一对一牵起来，牵上了才能比。
 *
 * 三个刻意的设计：
 * 1. 纯函数、不调模型。对比结论必须能在离线回放里一模一样地复现；
 *    要是让模型来判断¤这两句是不是同一件事¤，换一次模型就可能得出另一个结论，
 *    ¤比上次更好¤ 这种话就没有说服力了。
 * 2. 相似度用字符二元组 Jaccard。中文没有空格分词，二元组是零依赖里最稳的做法；
 *    它天生对语序不敏感、对虚词不敏感，正好对上¤改写只调措辞¤这个前提。
 * 3. 阈值是显式参数而不是魔数。改简历的力度可大可小，阈值给太高会让
 *    所有主张都变成¤新增¤，此时对比表看着很热闹、其实全是空的。
 */

import { VERDICT_LABEL } from './domain.ts';
import type { JudgedClaimLike } from './shortfall.ts';
import { WEAKNESS_RANK } from './shortfall.ts';

 */
export type ShiftKind = 'better' | 'same' | 'worse' | 'unjudged';

export const SHIFT_LABEL: Record<ShiftKind, string> = {
  better: '变好',
  same: '没变',
  worse: '变差',
  unjudged: '没判定',
};

/**
 * 默认阈值。定在 0.34 是拿真实简历试出来的：

 * 毫不相干的两条主张（一条写证书、一条写性能数字）几乎为 0；
 * 只用同一个主语、其余全不同的两条大约 0.2。0.34 落在后面那个空隙里。
 */
export const DEFAULT_ALIGN_THRESHOLD = 0.
```

Now design `growth.ts`. Let me think carefully about data model.

Input: a chain of sessions, ordered oldest → newest. Each: `{ id, createdAt, jobTitle, claims: JudgedClaimLike[], probes: number|null }`.

Output:
- `points`: per version — `{ sessionId, createdAt, jobTitle, judged, solid, probes, index }`
- `trails`: array of `ClaimTrail` — each trail is a sequence of `TrailStep { sessionId, versionIndex, claimId, text, verdict, shift }`. Trail needs identity: use `id` = first claim id + version? Better: `key` = `${versionIndex}:${claimId}` of first appearance.
- `persistent`: trails where steps.length >= 2 and last verdict !== 'solid' (and judged). Actually "改过还在被打回": appeared in ≥2 versions and the latest judged verdict is not solid. Also should flag if it appeared in >=2 and stayed weak.

Also `headline`, `summary` counts.

Design details:
- Track vertex: for version v, align prev(v-1) claims with v claims via alignClaims. pairs give prev↔next mapping. removed → trail ends. added → new trail starts.
- Use index maps: prevIndex → trailIndex (index into trails array), nextIndex → trailIndex.

Implementation:
```
for each version i:
  if i === 0: each claim starts new trail
  else: alignment = alignClaims(previous.claims, current.claims)
     for each pair: trail = trailOfPrev[pair.prev.id]; append step
     for each added: new trail
     for each removed: mark ended (nothing needed, just don't extend)
```
Track `prevTrailByClaimId: Map<string, number>`.

Step shift: for first step in trail, shift relative to null = 'unjudged'? Better: `shift: ShiftKind | null` where null for first appearance. Hmm, but we want "相对上一次是变好还是变差". For the first step, no previous → use `'unjudged'`? That would be confusing. Let's use `shift: ShiftKind | 'new'`? Simpler: `shift: ShiftKind | null` with null = 首次出现. Frontend shows「首次出现」. I'll do that, clean.

verdictShift(prev.verdict, next.verdict) gives 'unjudged' when either is null — that's meaningful (e.g. prev judged, next not judged).


```ts
/**
 * 成长档案（P12 · ②）...
 */

import type { VerdictKind } from './domain.ts';
import { VERDICT_LABEL } from './domain.ts';
import type { JudgedClaimLike } from './shortfall.ts';
import { WEAKNESS_RANK } from './shortfall.ts';
import type { ShiftKind } from './compare.ts';
import { alignClaims, verdictShift } from './compare.ts';

export interface GrowthSession {
  id: string;
  createdAt: string;
  jobTitle: string;
  claims: readonly JudgedClaimLike[];
  probes: number | null;
}

export interface GrowthPoint {
  sessionId: string;
  createdAt: string;
  jobTitle: string;
  /** 0 是第一版 */
  version: number;
  judged: number;
  solid: number;
  weak: number;
  probes: number | null;
}

export interface TrailStep {
  sessionId: string;
  version: number;
  claimId: string;
  text: string;
  verdict: VerdictKind | null;
  /** 相对上一次出现：null 表示首次出现 */
  shift: ShiftKind | null;
}

export interface ClaimTrail {
  key: string;
  firstVersion: number;
  lastVersion: number;
  versions: number;
  text: string;
  verdict: VerdictKind | null;
  presentInLatest: boolean;
  everSolid: boolean;
  persistent: boolean;
  steps: TrailStep[];
}

export interface GrowthView {
  versions: GrowthPoint[];
  trails: ClaimTrail[];
  persistent: ClaimTrail[];
  fixed: ClaimTrail[];
  single: number;
  headline: string;
  trailNote: string;
}
```

```text
import type { ClaimCategory, VerdictKind } from "./domain.ts";
import { CLAIM_CATEGORY_LABEL, VERDICT_LABEL } from "./domain.ts";

/**
 * 只取聚合真正用到的字段。
 * 服务端的 ClaimRow 与前端的 ClaimView 都能直接喂进来——两边各自多带的字段会被忽略。
 */
export interface JudgedClaimLike {
  id: string;
  text: string;
  category: ClaimCategory;
  /** null = 这一条还没判（跑一半、或判定失败） */
  verdict: VerdictKind | null;
}

/** 欠证据的程度：数字越大越该先补。solid 是「已经站得住」，排在最末、不进最该补的名单。 */
export const WEAKNESS_RANK: Record<VerdictKind, number> = {
  solid: 0,
  overstated: 1,
  unsupported: 2,
  contradicts: 3,

```

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

12 条全绿。现在服务层 `packages/server/src/growth.ts`：从这一场往上走 parentSessionId 到根，收集 sessions。

需要：`GET /api/sessions/:id/growth`。

返回结构 GrowthView 加上 chain 信息（versions 顺序、是否有断链说明）。

实现：
```ts
export const GROWTH_MAX_VERSIONS = 12;

export interface GrowthChain {
  rootId: string;
  /** 往上走时链断了（父会话被删） */
  broken: boolean;
  brokenAt: string | null;
  sessions: GrowthSession[];
}

export function growthChainOf(db, sessionId): GrowthChain | null
export function growthViewOf(db, sessionId): { chain + view } | null
```

沿 parent 往上走，最多 GROWTH_MAX_VERSIONS 版，防环（虽然结构上没有，但保险用 visited set）。

返回给前端：`{ sessionId, broken, brokenAt, window, ...GrowthView }`。

写吧。注意：`getSession` 返回 SessionRow；claims 用 listClaims 映射；probes 用 listProbes(...).length。

顺序：从这一场往上走到根，得到 latest→root 的数组，然后 reverse 成时间升序。

断链判定：走到的最后一个 session 有 parentSessionId 非空但 getSession 为 null → broken，brokenAt = 那个 id。

好。

</details>

```text
import { FAQ, HELP_STEPS, MODE_ROWS, PRIVACY_NOTE } from '../help.ts';
import { Chip } from './Bits.tsx';

/**
 * 新手教程：一屏讲完「这是什么 / 怎么用 / 三种模式区别 / 隐私」。
 * 做成覆盖层而不是新页面，是为了不打断手头那一场质询。
 */
export function HelpPanel(props: { open: boolean; onClose: () => void }) {
  if (!props.open) return null;
  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-start justify-center overflow-auto bg-ink-950/80 p-4 backdrop-blur-sm"
      data-shot="help"
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-label="新手教程"
      onClick={props.onClose}
    >
      <section
        className="pane my-4 w-full max-w-3xl p-5"
        onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
```

```text
/**
 * 「比上次」：把改完的简历再跑一遍，这一版比上一版怎么样。
 *
 * 这一栏刻意做成**可以收起来**的一条：
 * - 收起时只留一句话（status + headline），因为它常常是「0 → 1 条」这种一句话结论；
 * - 展开才把逐条变化摆出来，因为逐条列表会占掉右栏本来就不多的竖向空间。
 *
 * 四种状态（还没取到 / 第一版 / 上一版被删 / 真比出来了）的文案全在 compare.ts 里，
 * 这个组件只负责画。把判断留在纯函数里，是因为「第一版」与「上一版被删」
 * 在界面上必须说两句不一样的话，散在 JSX 里就一定会有一处写成同一个空状态。
 */
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import type { CompareView } from '../api.ts';
import { compareFactsOf, SHIFT_TONE } from '../compare.ts';
import { Badge, Pane } from './Bits.tsx';

export function CompareBar(props: { view: CompareView | null }) {
  const facts = compareFactsOf(props.view);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const rowCount = facts === null ? 0 : facts.rows.length;

  // 真比出了变好 / 变差的时候默认摊开：这正是点「改完再跑一遍」想看的东西。
  // 其他三种状态保持收起，免得一栏空话占着位置。
  useEffect(() => {
    if (rowCount > 0) setOpen(true);
  }, [rowCount]);

  if (facts === null) return null;

  const improved = facts.rows.filter((row) => row.shift === 'better').length;
  const worsened = facts.rows.filter((row) => row.shift === 'worse').length;

  return (
    <Pane
      title="比上次"
      dataShot="compare"
      className="max-h-[240px] shrink-0"
      subtitle={facts.status}
      actions={
```

```text
export function growthHeadline(view: Omit<GrowthView, 'headline' | 'trailNote'>): string {
  const total = view.versions.length;
  if (total === 0) return '还没有可以连成轨迹的场次。';
  if (total === 1) {
    return '目前只有 1 版：' + String(view.trails.length) + ' 条主张都在这一版上，轨迹要等第二版跑完才连得起来。';
  }
  const parts: string[] = [String(total) + ' 版、' + String(view.trails.length) + ' 条主张轨迹'];
  if (view.persistent.length > 0) parts.push('改了还在被打回 ' + String(view.persistent.length) + ' 条');
  if (view.fixed.length > 0) parts.push('已经改站住 ' + String(view.fixed.length) + ' 条');
  if (view.single > 0) parts.push('只出现过一版 ' + String(view.single) + ' 条');
  const latest = view.versions[view.versions.length - 1];
  if (latest !== undefined && latest.judged > 0) {
    parts.push('最新一版站得住 ' + String(latest.solid) + '/' + String(latest.judged) + ' 条');
  }
  return parts.join('，') + '。';
}

/** 给界面的一句话提示：没有轨迹可讲的时候必须说清楚为什么，不要留个空框 */
```

```text
async function sessions() {
  const raw = await (await fetch('http://127.0.0.1:' + PORT + '/api/sessions')).json();
  return Array.isArray(raw) ? raw : (raw.sessions || []);
}
async function waitSessions(count, timeoutMs) {
  const t0 = Date.now();
  for (;;) {
    const list = await sessions();
    if (list.length >= count) return list;
    if (Date.now() - t0 > timeoutMs) return list;
    await sleep(300);
  }
}
async function waitSessionsBelow(count, timeoutMs) {
  const t0 = Date.now();
  for (;;) {
    const list = await sessions();
    if (list.length <= count) return list;
    if (Date.now() - t0 > timeoutMs) return list;
    await sleep(300);
  }
}

---

255-    if (list.length <= count) return list;
256-    if (Date.now() - t0 > timeoutMs) return list;
257-    await sleep(300);
258-  }
259-}

```

```text
# ADR-0020：整场打包成一个文件，把「不许有外链」写成断言

- 状态：已采纳（2026-10-27，P11 的 C3）
- 相关：决策 D56、发现 F71/F72、`packages/server/src/bundle.ts`、`packages/server/src/http/app.ts`

## 背景

能导出的东西一直是四份：报告 md / html / doc，简历 txt / html / doc。自己带走够用，
但**发给别人不够用**——对方要收四个文件，还得自己判断哪个是哪个；发到微信里基本会散掉。
而这个产品最需要被传播的那一句话（「你简历里这句话，撑不住」）恰恰是在报告里的。

于是要一份「打包版」：一个文件，双击就看完整场。

## 决策

1. **报告正文不重写一份，直接嵌 `renderReportHtml` 的输出**。切掉它的 `<div class="footer">` 之前的部分，
   整段塞进打包文件，再在后面接两节附录。理由与 F63 同族：同一份事实两套渲染，
   迟早会长成两个版本——报告里说「变好 4 条」，打包里说「变好 3 条」，用户没法判断哪个是真的。
2. **「不许出现 http(s)://」是一条可执行的断言，不是文档里的一句话**。
   收件人的机器可能断网，一旦混进外链字体、统计脚本或图片，他看到的就是半张白纸，
   而这种失败在**发件人这边完全看不见**——他没有理由去点开自己刚发出去的东西。
   所以 `packages/server/test/bundle.test.ts` 里直接断言 `html.includes('http://') === false`，
   另外守一条 `<img|iframe ... src=>` 也不许出现。这条断言红了，等于「这个文件发出去会缺东西」。
3. **时间线只摆有意义的时刻**：`TIMELINE_KINDS` 收 stage / claims / requirements / probes /
   verdicts / suggestions / counter / error 八类；answers 与 budget 不单列（它们的后果已经体现在
   追问和判定里，逐条倒出来是没人读的日志）。每个阶段把 stage 键名翻成人话（抽取主张、读岗位要求……），
   键名是给开发者看的，读者不该见到 `jd-merge` 这种东西。
4. **简历那一节在读库之外自己重算**：`applyRevisions(view.session.resumeText, applied)` + `movedSpans`，
   与 `revisions.ts` 的 `toView` 走同一条路。不读 `revised_resume`
…（此处省略 2317 字符，原始长度 3517）
```
